Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1638); the archive.org record carries the Public Domain Mark (licenseurl publicdomain/mark/1.0)
Language: lat
Source: archive.org item bub_gb_cq-E0h5IwhAC, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-10 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (274,088) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1638, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: fair; the dates in the prologue are garbled. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: The Capuchins' own constitutions as confirmed under Urban VIII (title page at about OCR lines 18-33). The prologue at about 69-90 recalls the general chapter held in Rome at Sant'Eufemia in 153- (the last digit garbled) and the chapter of 1575.
